Firebase Backend (Security Rules \u0026 Cloud Functions)\n\n### `backend/firestore.rules`\n```javascript\nrules_version \u003d \u00272\u0027;\nservice cloud.firestore {\n  match /databases/{database}/documents {\n    function isSignedIn() {\n      return request.auth !\u003d null;\n    }\n\n    function isAdmin() {\n      return isSignedIn() \u0026\u0026 request.auth.token.admin \u003d\u003d true;\n    }\n\n    // Users can read public leaderboard fields; writes are handled via Cloud Functions\n    match /users/{uid} {\n      allow read: if isSignedIn();\n      allow write: if false;\n    }\n\n    // Wallets and coin transactions are read-only on client; mutated ONLY by Cloud Functions\n    match /wallets/{uid} {\n      allow read: if isSignedIn() \u0026\u0026 request.auth.uid \u003d\u003d uid;\n      allow write: if false;\n\n      match /transactions/{txId} {\n        allow read: if isSignedIn() \u0026\u0026 request.auth.uid \u003d\u003d uid;\n        allow write: if false;\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Categories, Questions, Coin Packages: readable by players, editable only by Admins\n    match /categories/{id} {\n      allow read: if true;\n      allow write: if isAdmin();\n    }\n\n    match /questions/{id} {\n      allow read: if isSignedIn();\n      allow write: if isAdmin();\n    }\n\n    match /coinPackages/{id} {\n      allow read: if true;\n      allow write: if isAdmin();\n    }\n\n    // Purchase audit logs \u0026 processed tokens: strictly server/admin only\n    match /purchaseAuditLogs/{id} {\n      allow read: if isAdmin();\n      allow write: if false;\n    }\n\n    match /processedPurchaseTokens/{tokenHash} {\n      allow read, write: if false;\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### `backend/functions/package.json`\n```json\n{\n  \"name\": \"quiz-master-pro-functions\",\n  \"scripts\": {\n    \"build\": \"tsc\",\n    \"serve\": \"npm run build \u0026\u0026 String(request.data?.purchaseToken ?? \"\");\n  const orderId \u003d String(request.data?.orderId ?? \"\");\n\n  if (!productId || !purchaseToken) {\n    throw new HttpsError(\"invalid-argument\", \"Missing productId or purchaseToken.\");\n  }\n\n  // 1. Lookup authoritative coin amount from Firestore coinPackages\n  const pkgQuery \u003d await db\n    .collection(\"coinPackages\")\n    .where(\"productId\", \"\u003d\u003d\", productId)\n    .where(\"isEnabled\", \"\u003d\u003d\", true)\n    .limit(1)\n    .get();\n\n  if (pkgQuery.empty) {\n    throw new HttpsError(\"not-found\", \"Unknown or disabled coin package.\");\n  }\n  const coinAmount \u003d Number(pkgQuery.docs[0].data().coinAmount ?? 0);\n\n  // 2. Verify purchase directly with Google Play Developer API\n  const authClient \u003d await google.auth.getClient({\n    scopes: [\"https://www.googleapis.com/auth/androidpublisher\"]\n  });\n  const androidPublisher \u003d google.androidpublisher({ version: \"v3\", auth: authClient });\n\n  const playRes \u003d await androidPublisher.purchases.products.get({\n    packageName: ANDROID_PACKAGE_NAME,\n    productId,\n    token: purchaseToken\n  });\n\n  // purchaseState: 0 \u003d Purchased, 1 \u003d Canceled, 2 \u003d Pending\n  if (playRes.data.purchaseState !\u003d\u003d 0) {\n    throw new HttpsError(\"failed-precondition\", \"Purchase is not completed.\");\n  }\n\n  // 3. Idempotent atomic credit + duplicate prevention\n  const tokenHash \u003d crypto.createHash(\"sha256\").update(purchaseToken).digest(\"hex\");\n  const tokenRef \u003d db.collection(\"processedPurchaseTokens\").doc(tokenHash);\n  const walletRef \u003d db.collection(\"wallets\").doc(uid);\n  const auditRef \u003d db.collection(\"purchaseAuditLogs\").doc();\n\n  await db.runTransaction(async (tx) \u003d\u003e {\n    const existingToken \u003d await tx.get(tokenRef);\n    if (existingToken.exists) {\n      throw new HttpsError(\"already-exists\", \"Duplicate purchase token rejected.\");\n    }\n\n    const walletSnap \u003d await tx.get(walletRef);\n    const currentCoins \u003d walletSnap.data()?.coinBalance ?? 0;\n\n    tx.set(tokenRef, { uid, productId, orderId, processedAt: Date.now() });\n    tx.set(walletRef, { coinBalance: currentCoins + coinAmount }, { merge: true });\n    tx.set(walletRef.collection(\"transactions\").doc(), {\n      type: \"PLAY_PURCHASE\",\n      amount: coinAmount,\n      title: `Coin Pack (${productId})`,\n      timestampMillis: Date.now(),\n      status: \"VERIFIED\",\n      orderId\n    });\n    tx.set(auditRef, {\n      uid,\n      productId,\n      coinAmount,\n      orderId,\n      status: \"VERIFIED\",\n      timestampMillis: Date.now()\n    });\n  });\n\n  // 4. Consume on Google Play so the user can repurchase the pack later\n  await androidPublisher.purchases.products.consume({\n    packageName: ANDROID_PACKAGE_NAME,\n    productId,\n    token: purchaseToken\n  });\n\n  return { coinsAdded: coinAmount };\n});\n```\n\n---\n\n## 9. Setup Instructions\n\n### A. Run the Complete Working Demo Immediately\n1. Open Android Studio and create/open the `QuizMasterPro` directory with the files above.\n2. Sync Gradle and run the app on an emulator or Android device (`minSdk 24+`).\n3. Out of the box (`AppConfig.DEMO_MODE \u003d true`, `AppConfig.REAL_PAYMENTS_ENABLED \u003d false`):\n   - Play quizzes across **Science, Technology, History, Sports, and General Knowledge** with **Easy, Medium, and Hard** difficulties and a **15-second timer**.\n   - Claim **Free Daily Rewards** and earn virtual coins from quizzes in the **Coin Wallet**.\n   - Inspect **Coin Shop** (`100`, `500`, `1,000`, `5,000` packages) with real payments safely disabled.\n   - Toggle **Demo Admin Role** inside the **Profile** screen to test the **Admin Panel** (add/edit/delete questions, toggle categories, manage coin packages, and view purchase history).\n\n### B. Connect Firebase (Auth, Firestore \u0026 Cloud Functions)\n1. Create a project in the Firebase Console and register Android app `com.quizmaster.pro` (include your debug/release SHA-1 fingerprints).\n2. Download `google-services.json` into `app/google-services.json`.\n3. Enable **Authentication → Google** in Firebase Console and copy your **Web client ID** into `app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml` (`default_web_client_id`).\n4. Deploy Firestore Rules and Cloud Functions from `backend/`:\n   ```bash\n   cd backend/functions\n   npm install\n   npm run build\n   firebase deploy --only firestore:rules,functions\n   ```\n5. Seed Firestore collections (`categories`, `questions`, `coinPackages`) and grant your admin account the `admin: true` custom claim via Firebase Admin SDK.\n6. Set `AppConfig.DEMO_MODE \u003d false` in `app/src/main/java/com/quizmaster/pro/core/config/AppConfig.kt`.\n\n### C. Enable Google Play Billing (Only When Ready \u0026 Approved)\n1. In Google Play Console, configure your Merchant Account, upload an internal/closed test release, and create the 4 Managed/One-Time In-App Products matching your product IDs:\n   - `coins_100`\n   - `coins_500`\n   - `coins_1000`\n   - `coins_5000`\n2. Grant your Firebase service account **Order management / View financial data** permissions in Play Console → *Users and permissions* (or *API access*) so `verifyPlayPurchase` can verify and consume tokens via the Google Play Developer API.\n3. Flip `REAL_PAYMENTS_ENABLED_SERVER \u003d true` in `backend/functions/src/index.ts`, redeploy functions, and set `AppConfig.REAL_PAYMENTS_ENABLED \u003d true` in `AppConfig.kt`. Google Play automatically handles approved payment methods including **Visa and Mastercard** without the app ever touching card data.",

        "text": " (result.responseCode) {\n            BillingClient.BillingResponseCode.OK -\u003e {\n                purchases.orEmpty().forEach { purchase -\u003e"
      }, {

Firebase Backend (Security Rules \u0026 Cloud Functions)\n\n### `backend/firestore.rules`\n```javascript\nrules_version \u003d \u00272\u0027;\nservice cloud.firestore {\n  match /databases/{database}/documents {\n    function isSignedIn() {\n      return request.auth !\u003d null;\n    }\n\n    function isAdmin() {\n      return isSignedIn() \u0026\u0026 request.auth.token.admin \u003d\u003d true;\n    }\n\n    // Users can read public leaderboard fields; writes are handled via Cloud Functions\n    match /users/{uid} {\n      allow read: if isSignedIn();\n      allow write: if false;\n    }\n\n    // Wallets and coin transactions are read-only on client; mutated ONLY by Cloud Functions\n    match /wallets/{uid} {\n      allow read: if isSignedIn() \u0026\u0026 request.auth.uid \u003d\u003d uid;\n      allow write: if false;\n\n      match /transactions/{txId} {\n        allow read: if isSignedIn() \u0026\u0026 request.auth.uid \u003d\u003d uid;\n        allow write: if false;\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Categories, Questions, Coin Packages: readable by players, editable only by Admins\n    match /categories/{id} {\n      allow read: if true;\n      allow write: if isAdmin();\n    }\n\n    match /questions/{id} {\n      allow read: if isSignedIn();\n      allow write: if isAdmin();\n    }\n\n    match /coinPackages/{id} {\n      allow read: if true;\n      allow write: if isAdmin();\n    }\n\n    // Purchase audit logs \u0026 processed tokens: strictly server/admin only\n    match /purchaseAuditLogs/{id} {\n      allow read: if isAdmin();\n      allow write: if false;\n    }\n\n    match /processedPurchaseTokens/{tokenHash} {\n      allow read, write: if false;\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### `backend/functions/package.json`\n```json\n{\n  \"name\": \"quiz-master-pro-functions\",\n  \"scripts\": {\n    \"build\": \"tsc\",\n    \"serve\": \"npm run build \u0026\u0026        status: \"COMPLETED\"\n      });\n    }\n  });\n\n  return { ok: true };\n});\n\nexport const claimDailyReward \u003d onCall(async (request) \u003d\u003e {\n  const uid \u003d already claimed today.\");\n    }\n\n    const nextStreak \u003d (data.dailyStreak ?? 0) + 1;\n    const bonus \u003d Math.min(nextStreak - 1, 5) * 10;\n    const reward \u003d DAILY_REWARD_BASE + bonus;\n\n    tx.set(\n      walletRef,\n      {\n        coinBalance: (data.coinBalance ?? 0) + reward,\n        lastDailyRewardAt: now,\n        dailyStreak: nextStreak\n      },\n      { merge: true }\n    );\n\n    tx.set(walletRef.collection(\"transactions\").doc(), {\n      type: \"DAILY_REWARD\",\n      amount: reward,\n      title: `Daily Reward (Day ${nextStreak})`,\n      timestampMillis: now,\n      status: \"COMPLETED\"\n    });\n\n    return reward;\n  });\n\n  return { coinsAdded };\n});\n\nexport const creditQuizReward \u003d onCall(async (request) \u003d\u003e {\n  const uid \u003d request.auth?.uid;\n  if (!uid) throw new HttpsError(\"unauthenticated\", \"Sign-in required.\");\n\n  const amount \u003d Number(request.data?.amount ?? 0);\n  const description \u003d String(request.data?.description ?? \"Quiz Reward\").slice(0, 80);\n\n  if (!Number.isFinite(amount) || amount \u003c 0 || amount \u003e 150) {\n    throw new HttpsError(\"invalid-argument\", \"Invalid quiz coin amount.\");\n  }\n  if (amount \u003d\u003d\u003d 0) return { coinsAdded: 0 };\n\n  const walletRef \u003d db.collection(\"wallets\").doc(uid);\n  const now \u003d Date.now();\n\n  await db.runTransaction(async (tx) \u003d\u003e {\n    const snap \u003d await tx.get(walletRef);\n    const current \u003d snap.data()?.coinBalance ?? 0;\n    tx.set(walletRef, { coinBalance: current + amount }, { merge: true });\n    tx.set(walletRef.collection(\"transactions\").doc(), {\n      type: \"QUIZ_REWARD\",\n      amount,\n      title: description,\n      timestampMillis: now,\n      status: \"COMPLETED\"\n    });\n  });\n\n  return { coinsAdded: amount };\n});\n\nexport const recordQuizProgress \u003d onCall(async (request) \u003d\u003e {\n  const uid \u003d request.auth?.uid;\n  if (!uid) throw new HttpsError(\"unauthenticated\", \""
      }, {
        "text": "Sign-in required.\");\n\n  const questionsAnswered \u003d Number(request.data?.questionsAnswered ?? 0);\n  const correctAnswers \u003d Number(request.data?.correctAnswers ?? 0);\n  const streakInSession \u003d Number(request.data?.streakInSession ?? 0);\n  const scoreEarned \u003d Number(request.data?.scoreEarned ?? 0);\n\n  if (questionsAnswered \u003c\u003d 0 || correctAnswers \u003c 0 || correctAnswers \u003e questionsAnswered) {\n    throw new HttpsError(\"invalid-argument\", \"Invalid quiz stats.\");\n  }\n\n  const userRef \u003d db.collection(\"users\").doc(uid);\n  await db.runTransaction(async (tx) \u003d\u003e {\n    const snap \u003d await tx.get(userRef);\n    const d \u003d snap.data() ?? {};\n    tx.set(\n      userRef,\n      {\n        totalQuizzesPlayed: (d.totalQuizzesPlayed ?? 0) + 1,\n        totalQuestionsAnswered: (d.totalQuestionsAnswered ?? 0) + questionsAnswered,\n        totalCorrectAnswers: (d.totalCorrectAnswers ?? 0) + correctAnswers,\n        bestStreak: Math.max(d.bestStreak ?? 0, streakInSession),\n        totalScore: (d.totalScore ?? 0) + Math.max(0, Math.min(scoreEarned, 5000))\n      },\n      { merge: true }\n    );\n  });\n\n  return { ok: true };\n});\n\n/**\n * Verifies Google Play Billing consumable coin purchases server-side.\n * Prevents duplicate transactions using processedPurchaseTokens/{sha256(token)}.\n */\nexport const verifyPlayPurchase \u003d onCall(async (request) \u003d\u003e {\n  const uid \u003d request.auth?.uid;\n  if (!uid) throw new HttpsError(\"unauthenticated\", \"Sign-in required.\");\n\n  if (!REAL_PAYMENTS_ENABLED_SERVER) {\n    throw new HttpsError(\n      \"failed-precondition\",\n      \"Real payments are disabled until Play Console \u0026 server verification are enabled.\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  const productId \u003d String(request.data?.productId ?? \"\");\n  const purchaseToken \u003d String(request.data?.purchaseToken ?? \"\");\n  const orderId \u003d String(request.data?.orderId ?? \"\");\n\n  if (!productId || !purchaseToken) {\n    throw new HttpsError(\"invalid-argument\", \"Missing productId or purchaseToken.\");\n  }\n\n  // 1. Lookup authoritative coin amount from Firestore coinPackages\n  const pkgQuery \u003d await db\n    .collection(\"coinPackages\")\n    .where(\"productId\", \"\u003d\u003d\", productId)\n    .where(\"isEnabled\", \"\u003d\u003d\", true)\n    .limit(1)\n    .get();\n\n  if (pkgQuery.empty) {\n    throw new HttpsError(\"not-found\", \"Unknown or disabled coin package.\");\n  }\n  const coinAmount \u003d Number(pkgQuery.docs[0].data().coinAmount ?? 0);\n\n  // 2. Verify purchase directly with Google Play Developer API\n  const authClient \u003d await google.auth.getClient({\n    scopes: [\"https://www.googleapis.com/auth/androidpublisher\"]\n  });\n  const androidPublisher \u003d google.androidpublisher({ version: \"v3\", auth: authClient });\n\n  const playRes \u003d await androidPublisher.purchases.products.get({\n    packageName: ANDROID_PACKAGE_NAME,\n    productId,\n    token: purchaseToken\n  });\n\n  // purchaseState: 0 \u003d Purchased, 1 \u003d Canceled, 2 \u003d Pending\n  if (playRes.data.purchaseState !\u003d\u003d 0) {\n    throw new HttpsError(\"failed-precondition\", \"Purchase is not completed.\");\n  }\n\n  // 3. Idempotent atomic credit + duplicate prevention\n  const tokenHash \u003d crypto.createHash(\"sha256\").update(purchaseToken).digest(\"hex\");\n  const tokenRef \u003d db.collection(\"processedPurchaseTokens\").doc(tokenHash);\n  const walletRef \u003d db.collection(\"wallets\").doc(uid);\n  const auditRef \u003d db.collection(\"purchaseAuditLogs\").doc();\n\n  await db.runTransaction(async (tx) \u003d\u003e {\n    const existingToken \u003d await tx.get(tokenRef);\n    if (existingToken.exists) {\n      throw new HttpsError(\"already-exists\", \"Duplicate purchase token rejected.\");\n    }\n\n    const walletSnap \u003d await tx.get(walletRef);\n    const currentCoins \u003d walletSnap.data()?.coinBalance ?? 0;\n\n    tx.set(tokenRef, { uid, productId, orderId, processedAt: Date.now() });\n    tx.set(walletRef, { coinBalance: currentCoins + coinAmount }, { merge: true });\n    tx.set(walletRef.collection(\"transactions\").doc(), {\n      type: \"PLAY_PURCHASE\",\n      amount: coinAmount,\n      title: `Coin Pack (${productId})`,\n      timestampMillis: Date.now(),\n      status: \"VERIFIED\",\n      orderId\n    });\n    tx.set(auditRef, {\n      uid,\n      productId,\n      coinAmount,\n      orderId,\n      status: \"VERIFIED\",\n      timestampMillis: Date.now()\n    });\n  });\n\n  // 4. Consume on Google Play so the user can repurchase the pack later\n  await androidPublisher.purchases.products.consume({\n    packageName: ANDROID_PACKAGE_NAME,\n    productId,\n    token: purchaseToken\n  });\n\n  return { coinsAdded: coinAmount };\n});\n```\n\n---\n\n## 9. Setup Instructions\n\n### A. Run the Complete Working Demo Immediately\n1. Open Android Studio and create/open the `QuizMasterPro` directory with the files above.\n2. Sync Gradle and run the app on an emulator or Android device (`minSdk 24+`).\n3. Out of the box (`AppConfig.DEMO_MODE \u003d true`, `AppConfig.REAL_PAYMENTS_ENABLED \u003d false`):\n   - Play quizzes across **Science, Technology, History, Sports, and General Knowledge** with **Easy, Medium, and Hard** difficulties and a **15-second timer**.\n   - Claim **Free Daily Rewards** and earn virtual coins from quizzes in the **Coin Wallet**.\n   - Inspect **Coin Shop** (`100`, `500`, `1,000`, `5,000` packages) with real payments safely disabled.\n   - Toggle **Demo Admin Role** inside the **Profile** screen to test the **Admin Panel** (add/edit/delete questions, toggle categories, manage coin packages, and view purchase history).\n\n### B. Connect Firebase (Auth, Firestore \u0026 Cloud Functions)\n1. Create a project in the Firebase Console and register Android app `com.quizmaster.pro` (include your debug/release SHA-1 fingerprints).\n2. Download `google-services.json` into `app/google-services.json`.\n3. Enable **Authentication → Google** in Firebase Console and copy your **Web client ID** into `app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml` (`default_web_client_id`).\n4. Deploy Firestore Rules and Cloud Functions from `backend/`:\n   ```bash\n   cd backend/functions\n   npm install\n   npm run build\n   firebase deploy --only firestore:rules,functions\n   ```\n5. Seed Firestore collections (`categories`, `questions`, `coinPackages`) and grant your admin account the `admin: true` custom claim via Firebase Admin SDK.\n6. Set `AppConfig.DEMO_MODE \u003d false` in `app/src/main/java/com/quizmaster/pro/core/config/AppConfig.kt`.\n\n### C. Enable Google Play Billing (Only When Ready \u0026 Approved)\n1. In Google Play Console, configure your Merchant Account, upload an internal/closed test release, and create the 4 Managed/One-Time In-App Products matching your product IDs:\n   - `coins_100`\n   - `coins_500`\n   - `coins_1000`\n   - `coins_5000`\n2. Grant your Firebase service account **Order management / View financial data** permissions in Play Console → *Users and permissions* (or *API access*) so `verifyPlayPurchase` can verify and consume tokens via the Google Play Developer API.\n3. Flip `REAL_PAYMENTS_ENABLED_SERVER \u003d true` in `backend/functions/src/index.ts`, redeploy functions, and set `AppConfig.REAL_PAYMENTS_ENABLED \u003d true` in `AppConfig.kt`. Google Play automatically handles approved payment methods including **Visa and Mastercard** without the app ever touching card data."
